We present BERT-LER, a BERT-style model for coded EHR timelines pretrained and fine-tuned from a de-identified EHR dataset of 75 million patients, that encodes laboratory test results as discrete tokens while retaining graded information through percentile-based binning, paired with Integrated Gradients for token-level attributions grounded in the input EHR sequence. We benchmark our approach on the public EHRShot benchmark suite and on an asthma severity progression study based on real-world data. This addresses a methodological gap in EHR foundation-style modeling by unifying laboratory value representation and explainability in a single framework, while assessing whether both predictive performance and explanations generalize beyond standard clinical prediction tasks. Across EHRShot and asthma tasks, BERT-LER achieves predictive performance that is competitive with, and on laboratory-related tasks often exceeds, publicly available benchmark models, and provides attributions that align with clinically known risk factors. Our architecture and explainability approach can be applied to many therapeutic areas and prediction tasks using language models trained on structured EHRs.

</details>


### [173] [Towards On-Board Implementation of ML-Based Helicopter Weight Estimator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19210)
*Nicolas Valot,Ammar Mechouche,Benjamin Lesage,Claire Pagetti,Louis Fabre*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于长短期记忆循环神经网络（LSTM RNN）的监督机器学习模型，用于直升机起飞重量估计，并依据EASA和Eurocae ED-324标准完成了学习保证流程，验证了模型在遗留航空电子计算机上的部署可行性，适用于机载关键功能如警报系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决直升机起飞重量实时准确估计的需求，利用空客全球在役机队的大量历史数据，开发符合航空认证标准（EASA、Eurocae ED-324）的机器学习模型，以支持机载关键功能（如起飞重量警报）的部署。

Method: 1. 收集空客全球在役直升机队的海量飞行数据作为训练集。2. 依据EASA机器学习应用概念文件和Eurocae ED-324制定机器学习需求、模型描述及实现规范。3. 设计并实现长短期记忆循环神经网络（LSTM RNN）作为重量估计模型。4. 在遗留航空电子计算机上进行需求验证与部署测试。

Result: 成功构建并验证了符合航空认证要求的LSTM RNN重量估计模型，验证结果显示模型在遗留航空电子硬件上运行稳定，满足机载关键功能（如起飞重量警报）的实时性与可靠性指标。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了基于LSTM RNN的监督学习模型可通过完整的学习保证流程，实现直升机起飞重量的高精度估计，并可部署于现有航空电子平台，为机载关键功能提供数据支持，推动了机器学习在航空安全关键系统中的工程化应用。

Abstract: This paper focuses on the implementation of a novel supervised Machine Learning model for estimating helicopter weight during takeoff, utilizing extensive datasets from Airbus's global in-service fleet. The study details a learning assurance process aligned with the EASA concept paper for machine learning application, and with the on-going Eurocae ED-324. We propose a set of Machine Learning Requirements, a Machine Learning Model Description, and its implementation for a long short-term memory recurrent neural network. Finally, we verify the requirements on the implementation. Demonstrated on legacy avionics computers, the implementation is suitable for the deployment of the developed Machine Learning Model weight estimator on airborne targets for critical functions such as on-board alerting.

</details>


### [174] [A comparison between ceiling-mounted FMCW, IR-UWB and Wi-Fi radar for in-bedroom human activity monitoring and sleep interruption detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20322)
*Anton Lambrecht,Reda El Hail,Xianjun Jiao,Pieter Crombez,Dominique Schreurs,Peter Karsmakers,Adnan Shahid,Eli De Poorter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在相同部署条件下对比了天花板安装的 FMCW 雷达、IR-UWB 和 Wi-Fi 感知在医疗监测中的表现，发现 IR-UWB 在跨受试者活动识别上最优，FMCW 在未见房间布局泛化上最优，睡眠监测三者均超 92% F1，揭示了识别性能与环境鲁棒性的权衡，为医疗 RF 感知系统设计提供指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究在硬件、数据集和评估方法上差异大，导致不同射频技术难以在相同部署条件下公平对比；且天花板安装雷达在医疗环境中具有部署和成本优势，但其性能尚未充分探索。

Method: 收集 20 名参与者在 6 种房间布局下的同步记录，使用相同的卷积神经网络（CNN）分别在细粒度 10 类人体活动识别（HAR）和粗粒度 4 类睡眠监测任务上评估 FMCW、IR-UWB 和 Wi-Fi 感知。

Result: IR-UWB 在跨受试者活动识别上达到最高宏平均 F1 89.0%；FMCW 在未见房间布局泛化上达到最高宏平均 F1 83.8%；睡眠监测任务中，三种技术在未见环境下均超过 92% 宏平均 F1。结果揭示了识别性能与环境鲁棒性的根本权衡，可通过距离分辨率、天线分集、多普勒分辨率和空间信息保留差异来解释。

Conclusion: 研究为医疗导向的 RF 感知系统设计提供了实用指南：根据应用场景优先考虑跨受试者识别性能（选 IR-UWB）或环境泛化能力（选 FMCW），睡眠监测任务三种技术均可胜任。

Abstract: Despite their growing importance for contact-free radio frequency (RF) based healthcare monitoring, different radio technologies such as frequency-modulated continuous wave (FMCW) radar, impulse radio ultra-wideband (IR-UWB), and Wi-Fi sensing are rarely compared under identical deployment conditions, as existing studies typically differ in hardware, datasets, and evaluation methodologies. In addition, the performance of ceiling-mounted radars, despite their practical deployment and cost advantages in healthcare environments, remain underexplored. Therefore, this paper presents a controlled comparison and analysis of ceiling-mounted FMCW, IR-UWB, and Wi-Fi sensing using synchronized recordings from 20 participants across six room layouts. All technologies are evaluated with the same convolutional neural network (CNN) on both a fine-grained 10-class human activity recognition (HAR) task and a coarse 4-class sleep monitoring task. IR-UWB achieves the highest cross-subject activity recognition performance (89.0% macro F1), while FMCW generalizes best to unseen room layouts (83.8% macro F1). For sleep monitoring, all technologies exceed 92% macro F1 in unseen environments. The results reveal a fundamental trade-off between recognition performance and environmental robustness, which can be explained through differences in range resolution, antenna diversity, Doppler resolution, and spatial information retention. These findings provide practical guidelines for the design of healthcare-oriented RF sensing systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [175] [SSR-GRPO: Integrating Supervision and Semantic IDs into Reinforcement Learning for Dense Retrieval in E-commerce](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19595)
*Guangxin Song,Xing Fang,Mingmin Jin,Jing Wang,Bokang Wang,Zhentao Song,Junjie Bai,Jianbo Zhu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出 SSR-GRPO 方法，结合语义标识符和密集向量的双视角相关性评估，利用硬负样本挖掘设计掩码函数和检索 DPO 任务，解决 EBR 中复杂语义建模和 R-GRPO 的噪声与偏见问题，已在大规模电商平台部署并验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于嵌入的检索在电商搜索中难以处理复杂隐含语义；微调大语言模型进行表示学习缺乏鲁棒机制；R-GRPO 虽引入强化学习，但受限于批采样导致 top-K 候选噪声大，且使用同质 LLM 作为奖励模型带来相关性评估偏见。

Method: 1. 提出双视角相关性评估框架：融合量化学习生成的语义标识符（SIDs）和密集表示向量，产生更无偏的相关性分数。2. 利用 SIDs 的层级相似性挖掘硬负样本：(a) 设计掩码函数集成进 R-GRPO，过滤组内噪声样本；(b) 构建检索 DPO 任务，通过正负样本对捕捉细粒度语义区分。3. 整合上述策略形成 SSR-GRPO。

Result: 大量离线和在线实验验证 SSR-GRPO 的有效性，并已在大规模电商平台成功部署。

Conclusion: SSR-GRPO 通过语义标识符辅助的双视角评估和硬负样本驱动的掩码与 DPO 优化，有效解决了复杂语义建模、噪声候选及奖励偏见问题，显著提升了电商搜索的检索性能并实现了工业级落地。

Abstract: Embedding-based retrieval (EBR) is pivotal in e-commerce search but often struggles with complex semantics. While recent methods often fine-tune large language models (LLMs) for representation learning, they typically lack robust mechanisms for handling complex and implicit semantics. While Retrieval-GRPO (R-GRPO) recently introduced reinforcement learning to dense retrieval, it suffers from noisy top-K candidates due to limited batch sampling and biased relevance assessments caused by using similarly trained LLMs as reward models. To tackle these issues, we propose Supervised Retrieval-GRPO with Semantic Identifiers (SSR-GRPO). Specifically, our method first proposes a dual-perspective framework for relevance assessment. It leverages both Semantic Identifiers (SIDs) produced by quantization learning and dense representation vectors to generate more unbiased relevance scores. Furthermore, leveraging the hierarchical similarity relationships of the generated SIDs, we mine a set of hard negative samples that serve two purposes: (1) to design a masking function integrated into R-GRPO, effectively filtering intra-group noisy samples; and (2) to construct a Retrieval-DPO task composed of positive and negative sample pairs, enabling the model to capture fine-grained semantic distinctions from a pair-wise perspective. By integrating these optimization strategies, we propose SSR-GRPO. Extensive offline and online experiments validate SSR-GRPO's effectiveness, and it has been deployed on a large-scale e-commerce platform.

</details>


### [176] [Training-Free LLM-Based Recommendation with Post-LLM Item Refinement Using Collaborative Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19665)
*Kyungho Kim,Sunwoo Kim,Geon Lee,Shinhwan Kang,Sojeong Kim,Liam Collins,Bhuvesh Kumar,Donald Loveland,Kijung Shin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: CoRRe 是一种无需训练的推荐框架，采用后 LLM 范式将协同过滤信号注入 LLM 生成的物品表示中，再与用户兴趣匹配进行排序，实现了优于现有无训练方法且媲美有训练方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 生成的用户兴趣往往过于宽泛，难以支撑细粒度物品检索；现有方法在 LLM 前引入 CF 信号（如候选重排序或提示增强），增益有限。

Method: 提出 CoRRe 框架：利用物品-物品共购图修正物品嵌入的方向，利用物品流行度调整嵌入的幅度，随后将优化后的物品表示与 LLM 生成的用户兴趣匹配以进行排序。

Result: 在真实数据集上，CoRRe 持续超越现有无训练推荐方法，且性能达到或超过有训练方法，同时完全不需要模型训练或任务特定微调。

Conclusion: 后 LLM 范式注入 CF 信号能有效弥补 LLM 兴趣表示的粗糙性，CoRRe 证明了无训练推荐可兼顾效果与效率，为 LLM 推荐提供了新范式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown promise for training-free recommendation, but LLM-generated user interests are often too broad for fine-grained item retrieval. Existing methods incorporate collaborative filtering (CF) signals in a pre-LLM manner through candidate reranking or prompt augmentation, yielding limited gains. We propose CoRRe, a training-free recommendation framework with a post-LLM paradigm that injects CF signals into LLM-generated item representations, which are later matched with LLM-generated user interests for ranking. Specifically, CoRRe refines the directions of item embeddings using an item-item co-purchase graph and their magnitudes using item popularity. Experiments on real-world datasets show that CoRRe consistently outperforms existing training-free methods and achieves competitive or superior performance compared with training-based methods, without requiring any model training or task-specific fine-tuning.

</details>


### [177] [Do Sequential Recommendation Benchmarks Really Require Higher-Order Sequence Modelling?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19833)
*Aleksandr V. Petrov,Praveen Chandar,Paul N. Bennett,Hugues Bouchard,Mounia Lalmas*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 简单的基于近期加权的成对探针在多个基准数据集上优于Transformer-based序列推荐模型，表明这些基准不适合衡量高阶序列建模的增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究广泛使用的基准是否真正需要高阶序列建模能力，因为目前的序列推荐模型越来越多地使用语言模型架构来捕捉复杂的上下文相关交互，但尚不清楚基准是否真的需要这种建模能力。

Method: 使用两个简单的、基于近期加权的成对探针（Sequential Rules和Probabilistic Collaborative Transition Model），它们不学习高阶序列表示，并遵循eSASRec的评估协议进行对比。

Result: 在三个Amazon数据集上，至少一个探针比eSASRec复现版高15-38%；在MovieLens-1M上高4.4%；但在MovieLens-20M上落后27.3%。在其余四个数据集上，至少一个探针比采样softmax SASRec复现版高9-28%。

Conclusion: 这些广泛使用的基准不适合衡量高阶序列建模的增益；将Transformer-based模型与强基于近期加权的成对探针对比，提供了一个具体的测试，以判断基准是否能有意义地衡量高阶序列建模的增益。

Abstract: Sequential recommenders increasingly use language-model architectures designed to capture complex, context-dependent interactions. Yet it remains unclear whether widely used benchmarks actually require this modelling capacity. We investigate this question using two simple, recency-weighted pairwise probes that do not learn higher-order sequence representations: Sequential Rules (SeqRules) and our Probabilistic Collaborative Transition Model (PCTM). Using the evaluation protocol of eSASRec, at least one probe exceeds our eSASRec reproduction by 15-38% on three Amazon datasets and by 4.4% on MovieLens-1M, but trails it by 27.3% on MovieLens-20M. On the four remaining datasets, at least one probe also outperforms our sampled-softmax SASRec reproduction by 9-28%, suggesting that these widely used benchmarks are poorly suited to measuring gains from higher-order sequence modelling. More broadly, comparing Transformer-based models against strong recency-weighted pairwise probes provides a concrete test of whether a benchmark can meaningfully measure gains from higher-order sequence modelling.

</details>


### [178] [SCoRD: Semantic-Assisted Continual Retriever-Reranker Distillation for LLM-Based Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19998)
*Seunghyun Baek,Gyuseok Lee,Seunghan Lee,Wonbin Kweon,Dong Wang,SeongKu Kang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出SCoRD，一个用于非平稳数据流下基于LLM重排序流水线的持续知识蒸馏框架。它引入语义推理助手将LLM推断用户意图的能力蒸馏为可复用的意图级指导，选择性地将重排序器知识蒸馏给检索器，指导仅检索器的更新而无需重复LLM推理，并将检索器派生的表示和意图漂移信号反馈给重排序器，实现高效的检索器-重排序器协同自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统采用两阶段流水线（ID检索器+LLM重排序器），需持续适应兴趣演变和新交互。朴素方案是反复更新LLM重排序器并蒸馏，成本过高；仅更新检索器虽便宜，但容量有限难以从稀疏数据适应。因此需要一种高效的持续知识蒸馏框架，实现检索器与重排序器的协同自适应。

Method: SCoRD包含三个核心组件：1) 语义推理助手：将LLM推断潜在用户意图的能力蒸馏为可复用的意图级指导；2) 选择性知识蒸馏：仅在低置信度序列上将重排序器知识蒸馏给检索器；3) 双向反馈机制：指导仅检索器更新无需重复LLM推理，并将检索器派生的表示和意图漂移信号反馈给重排序器。

Result: 在真实世界数据集上的实验表明，SCoRD能够实现有效且高效的检索器-重排序器协同自适应，优于基线方法。

Conclusion: SCoRD通过语义推理助手、选择性蒸馏和双向反馈，解决了非平稳数据流下LLM重排序流水线的持续适应问题，以较低成本实现了检索器与重排序器的协同进化，为工业界部署提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Recommendation systems increasingly adopt a two-stage pipeline, where an ID-based retriever retrieves candidates and an LLM-based reranker refines their rankings. To improve retrieval quality, reranker-to-retriever distillation is commonly used to transfer the reranker's knowledge to the retriever. For practical deployment, however, this pipeline must continually adapt to evolving interests and incoming interactions. A naive solution is to repeatedly update the LLM reranker and distill its latest knowledge, but this incurs prohibitive costs. Updating the retriever alone is cheaper, but its limited capacity makes adaptation from sparse data difficult. We propose SCoRD, a continual knowledge distillation framework for LLM-based reranking pipelines under a non-stationary data stream. SCoRD introduces a semantic reasoning assistant that distills the LLM's ability to infer underlying user intents into reusable intent-level guidance. It selectively distills reranker knowledge to the retriever on low-confidence sequences, guides retriever-only updates without repeated LLM inference, and feeds retriever-derived representations and intent-drift signals back to the reranker. Experiments on real-world datasets show that SCoRD enables effective and efficient retriever-reranker co-adaptation.

</details>


### [179] [Daedalus-150M: A Convolution-Attention Hybrid Designed for CPU Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20210)
*Christos Koutsiaris*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种面向CPU推理的小型语言模型架构，采用混合注意力与短卷积设计，在18层中仅6层使用全注意力，其余12层使用固定两步宽度的短卷积，避免了KV缓存随对话增长。模型在59.9B tokens上从头训练，以4位量化权重，在五任务基准上得分47.31，显著优于同规模甚至更大数据训练的GPT-2、Pythia、OPT、GPT-neo及MobileLLM。消融实验表明混合架构在同等数据下比全注意力模型质量高0.81%，4位模型体积减小6.3%，解码速度随上下文长度增长优势扩大（2048 tokens时快1.76倍），且非仅由带宽节省解释。同时报告了4位量化质量损失、约半数卷积通道失活及词表过大等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有小模型通常先按大模型方式构建再压缩至CPU，作者反其道而行之：先固定目标场景（单用户、逐token生成、4位权重、普通CPU），再设计适配架构，旨在解决小模型在CPU上推理时内存带宽与缓存增长的瓶颈。

Method: 设计混合架构：18个Transformer块中仅6个保留全注意力，其余12个替换为短卷积，卷积内存固定为两个时间步宽度，不随上下文增长。模型采用4位量化权重，从头在59.9B tokens上训练。对比实验：同规模、同数据训练全注意力基线，预先设定胜出条件，评估质量、下游任务、4位模型大小、不同上下文长度解码速度。

Result: 混合模型在五任务基准得分47.31（基线42.20），击败GPT-2 124M、Pythia-160M、OPT-125M、GPT-neo-125M（后者训练数据3-6倍）及MobileLLM-125M（万亿tokens）。验证集bits-per-byte 0.8685。对比全注意力基线：质量指标胜0.81%，下游任务持平，4位文件减小6.3%，2048 tokens上下文解码快1.76倍，对外部同规模模型快2.08倍。速度优势随上下文增长，带宽计算仅预测1.17倍，说明非仅内存体积优势。负面结果：4位量化带来不可缓解的质量损失，约半数卷积通道失活且无法移除，词表规模超出模型容量合理范围。

Conclusion: 面向CPU推理的约束驱动架构设计可显著提升小模型效能：混合注意力与短卷积在极小数据量下实现SOTA质量，且解码速度随上下文增长优势放大，验证了机制预测。但4位量化质量损失、卷积通道利用率低及词表过大等问题仍需进一步优化。

Abstract: Small language models are usually built like large ones and then squeezed onto a CPU afterwards. We did the opposite: we fixed the target first, one user, one token at a time, 4-bit weights, ordinary CPU, and chose the architecture to suit it. The result keeps full attention in only 6 of its 18 blocks. The other 12 use short convolutions whose memory is two timesteps wide no matter how long the conversation gets, so two thirds of the network never re-reads a growing cache.
  Trained from scratch on 59.9B tokens, the model scores 47.31 on a five-task benchmark against a bar of 42.20 that was fixed before training began. It beats GPT-2 124M, Pythia-160M, OPT-125M and GPT-neo-125M, all trained on three to six times more data, and exceeds MobileLLM-125M's published score despite that model seeing a trillion tokens. Validation bits-per-byte is 0.8685.
  To check the architecture rather than the training recipe, we trained a conventional all-attention model of the same size on the same data, and wrote down the winning condition before scoring either. The hybrid won the chosen quality metric by 0.81%, matched it on downstream tasks, produced a 6.3% smaller 4-bit file, and decoded 1.76x faster at 2048 tokens of context, 2.08x against an external model of similar size. In every measurement the speed advantage is near zero at an empty context and grows with length, which is what the mechanism predicts and what a merely leaner model would not show. A simple bandwidth calculation predicts only 1.17x, so memory volume alone does not explain the gap.
  We also report what did not work: an unmitigated 4-bit quality cost, roughly half the convolution channels ending up inert and impossible to remove, and a vocabulary larger than this model size warrants.

</details>


### [180] [What Makes a Good Fiqh Retriever? Answer Retrieval for Arabic Islamic Jurisprudence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20246)
*Somaya Eltanbouly,Heba Sbahi,Samer Rashwani,Abdessalam Bouchekif,Mutaz al-Khatib,Shahd Gaben,Mohammed Ghaly*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文研究阿拉伯语教法（fiqh）问答中的答案承载检索，构建了专门的检索测试集，评估了密集、词法、混合、微调和教法学派感知等检索策略，发现微调能提升性能，教法学派感知过滤在学派特定问题上将MRR@5提升一倍以上，主要挑战是区分答案承载段落与主题相似但不含裁决的段落。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有伊斯兰问答系统多采用端到端评估，难以将检索失败与生成失败分离；且教法问答要求段落必须包含问题所需的具体裁决，而非仅主题相关，因此需要专门研究答案承载检索并建立相应评测基准。

Method: 构建阿拉伯语教法检索测试集，系统评估五类检索策略：密集检索、词法检索、混合检索、微调检索器、教法学派感知过滤；使用MRR@5作为主要评价指标，并进行错误分析。

Result: 最佳基础检索器达到0.524 MRR@5；微调将性能提升至0.553；混合检索对强模型增益有限；教法学派感知过滤在学派特定问题上使MRR@5提升一倍以上；错误分析显示核心难点是区分包含裁决的答案承载段落与仅主题相似的段落。

Conclusion: 教法问答中的答案承载检索需要专门建模；微调能有效提升检索性能；引入教法学派知识的显式过滤机制对学派特定问题极其关键；未来工作应聚焦于更精细地识别包含具体裁决的段落。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation is used for Islamic question answering, but most systems are evaluated end-to-end, making retrieval failures difficult to isolate from generation failures. We study answer-bearing retrieval for Arabic fiqh, where a passage is relevant only if it states the ruling required by the question. We build a retrieval test collection for Arabic fiqh and use it to evaluate dense, lexical, hybrid, fine-tuned, and madhhab-aware retrieval strategies. The best retriever achieves 0.524 MRR@5, while fine-tuning improves performance to 0.553. Hybrid retrieval provides limited gains for strong models, whereas madhhab-aware filtering more than doubles MRR@5 on school-specific questions. We further present an error analysis showing that the main challenge is distinguishing answer-bearing passages from topically similar passages that do not contain the requested ruling.

</details>


### [181] [Projecting BrowseComp-Plus onto ClimbMix: Toward More Realistic Corpora for Agentic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20317)
*Sahel Sharifymoghaddam,Lingwei Gu,Yijun Ge,Jimmy Lin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出 BrowseComp-Plus_CM 基准，将原 BrowseComp-Plus 的问题证据迁移到大规模预训练语料 ClimbMix（400B token，5.53 亿文档），并设计投影管线将问题分解为原子推理跳并验证每一跳均有证据支持，最终得到 57 个完全 grounded 的问题。实验显示投影将难度转移到检索端，最强智能体答案准确率下降 5 分，证据召回从 84.3% 跌至 21.4%，搜索调用增加 63%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原 BrowseComp-Plus 语料仅 10 万文档，且证据和干扰项均按查询选取，存在数据泄露和评估偏差；需将问题证据迁移到与基准无关的大规模真实网络语料（ClimbMix），以更真实地评估智能体搜索的检索能力。

Method: 设计数据集无关的投影管线：将每个问题分解为原子推理跳，在 ClimbMix 中为每一跳定位支持证据，经自动验证、独立智能体验证和人工复核三重确认后保留问题；最终获得 57 个完全 grounded 的测试问题及相关性判断。

Result: 在 830 个原测试问题中，管线产出 57 个高质量问题。投影后，最强智能体答案准确率下降 5 个百分点，证据召回从 84.3% 锐减至 21.4%，搜索调用次数增加 63%，表明难度显著转移到检索环节。

Conclusion: 投影管线可将现有推理基准投影到大规模无偏语料，构建更具挑战性的检索评估基准；发布了管线、基准及分析，为后续研究提供资源。

Abstract: The BrowseComp-Plus benchmark disentangled the evaluation of agentic search by replacing opaque web search with a fixed corpus, so that an agent's role can be separated from the retriever's. That corpus, however, holds only about 100K documents and was assembled from the supporting documents of the benchmark's own queries plus mined hard negatives, so the evidence and the distractors were both selected per query. We introduce $\text{BrowseComp-Plus}_{\text{CM}}$, which keeps the BrowseComp-Plus questions but relocates their evidence to ClimbMix, a 400B-token, 553M-document mixture of web text released by NVIDIA for pre-training language models and built without reference to any benchmark. Our main contribution is the projection pipeline that makes this possible: it decomposes each question into atomic reasoning hops and grounds every hop in the new corpus, retaining a question only when automatic verification, an independent agent, and human review all confirm that every hop is supported. The pipeline is dataset-agnostic and applies to any benchmark whose questions decompose into verifiable facts. Applied to the 830 BrowseComp-Plus test questions, our pipeline yields 57 fully grounded questions with question-level relevance judgments. Projection shifts the difficulty onto retrieval, as the strongest agent we evaluate loses five points of answer accuracy but sees its evidence recall fall from 84.3% to 21.4% while issuing 63% more search calls. As the first of a series of projections, we release the pipeline, the benchmark, and our analyses at https://github.com/castorini/cmass.

</details>


### [182] [HARP: Hierarchical Adaptive Ranking with Preference-Adaptive Fusion for Query-Based CVE Prioritization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19430)
*Haochen Liu,Zhengzhang Chen,Haoyu Wang,Yanchi Liu,Jundong Li,Haifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: HARP是一个基于图的多视图框架，用于CVE漏洞优先级排序，它结合自然语言查询和历史标记示例，无需显式偏好场景描述即可进行有效的漏洞优先级排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 漏洞优先级排序本质上依赖于偏好，同一CVE在不同操作偏好场景下可能获得不同的修复优先级。现有评分系统通常假设固定标准，而组织实际运行时偏好往往是隐性的、难以用文本提示表达的，但当前偏好场景下的历史验证分类案例更容易获取。

Method: HARP从漏洞知识图谱检索证据，利用策略条件下的全局视图、企业视图和用户视图对候选漏洞进行评分，并从采样的支持集中拟合视图融合权重，实现基于查询的CVE优先级排序。

Result: 在三种偏好场景和多个主干LLM上的实验表明，HARP优于多个基线方法，验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: HARP通过利用历史标记案例而非显式偏好描述，有效解决了查询式CVE优先级排序中偏好隐性化、难以表达的问题，为漏洞管理提供了更实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Vulnerability prioritization is inherently preference dependent, since the same CVE can receive different remediation priority under different operational preference scenarios. Existing scoring systems and ranking methods typically assume a fixed criterion. In practice, organizations already operate under a preference scenario, but this preference is often implicit and difficult to express as a written prompt instruction, while triage queries usually do not encode it. Past validated triage cases under the current scenario are more readily available. We study query-based CVE prioritization in this setting and propose HARP, a graph-grounded multi-view framework that ranks candidates from a natural-language query together with a support bank of historical labeled examples from the current preference scenario, without requiring an explicit textual summary of that scenario. HARP retrieves evidence from a vulnerability knowledge graph, scores candidates with policy-conditioned global, enterprise, and user views, and fits view-fusion weights from sampled supports. Experiments across three preference scenarios and multiple backbone LLMs show that HARP outperforms multiple baselines, expressing our method's effectiveness.

</details>
